Your job: after 24 h, audit its paper trades: n, per £10, hit rate, ticks, by price band/venue/secs-to-off; check fills are 'through' not 'touch'; check no in-play entries; standard verdict. Original spec: **Racing pre-off scalper, Caan Berry / Peter Webb rules, PAPER on the live stream.** Build /root/pe-race/preoff_scalp_paper.py in the style of /root/pe-race/race_mm_size.py (read it first for the stream_ticks access pattern, heartbeat and keepalive conventions). Rules, exact: UK/IE WIN markets only, last 10 minutes before the off, total matched >= £50k at entry, runner price 2.0–8.0, book spread <= 2 ticks. Weight of money over a rolling 30 s on the first three price points each side: when back-side money / (back+lay) >= 0.65 and the last 3 traded prices are not lengthening, BACK at the touch; when <= 0.35 and not shortening, LAY at the touch. Exit: +2 ticks (lay back out / back back out) or −2 ticks stop, or 60 s elapsed, or 30 s before the off, whichever first; one position per runner at a time, max 3 open positions, never in play. Fills: paper fill only when the touch price trades THROUGH your level (not touched). Table preoff_scalp_paper (ts, market_id, venue, sel, side, entry_px, exit_px, exit_reason, ticks, pnl_per_10), heartbeat preoff_scalp_heartbeat, keepalive via nohup. No orders of any kind. Report after 24 h: n, per £10, hit rate, by price band and by venue.

Segunda's Under 2.5 won 3.7–4.4 points less often than priced in both seasons (z about −2.5) and Serie B 2.0–2.7, while Championship, Ligue 2 and 2. Bundesliga sit within about 1 point; but those lines were picked on both seasons, so they cannot prove themselves. Rule: for every league on the tape, overround-removed Under 2.5 at the first print v the result, as in 499; rank correlation of the 2024 error against the 2025 error across leagues; then, choosing on 2024 only, lay Under 2.5 in leagues with error <= −2 points and back it in leagues >= +2 points, and trade 2025 only, one bet per game, one tick adverse. A single season cannot meet the two-half rule, so split 2025 by median date. If 2024 does not predict 2025, the Segunda lead is selection and is closed. LTP only, so a pass is an upper bound and then needs the real touch on liq_sample.

Test: places paid from the market name where it says ("2 TBP") or the winners in the race; sum of 1/BSP against places paid by field size; then back and lay at BSP by places paid x field, one tick adverse, also exact BSP; choose on data before Jul 2025 and score after; one entry per race per state. Note that winners counted from the result are safe only if the terms were known before the off, so check them against the name.

Rule fixed before any price was read: scheduled market_time = T, no look at the real off. Trigger: first pre-off print in T-90 to T-5 at least X% (3, 5, 10) away from the same runner's latest print 30 s to 5 min earlier. One entry per market, first trigger. Entry: the next print of that runner within 120 s, must be pre-off, worse of trigger and next, one tick adverse, price 1.10-20, no next print = no trade, counted. FOLLOW backs a shortener and lays a drifter, FADE the reverse. Exit: first print 10+ min after entry and pre-off, else the last pre-off print after entry, else the first in-play print, one tick adverse, 2% commission on the green. No exit print = counted and reported. Lines: sport x X x side x (all, T-90/-45, T-45/-15, T-15/-5, price under 1.5, 1.5-2.5, 2.5-20), 84, plus a control at the first print after T-30. Rule fixed before prices are read. Early window: reference = last print T-45 to T-20, signal = last print T-15 to T-10; late window: reference = last print T-20 to T-10, signal = last print T-8 to T-5. SHORT signal/reference <= 0.90, DRIFT >= 1.10, FLAT 0.98-1.02 is a control, not gated. Entry = first print after the signal print, by T-5 (early) or T-3 (late), worse of it and the next print within 120 s, one tick adverse; signal print is never the entry, so bounce cannot make the signal. Exit = last pre-off print after entry, one tick adverse. Entry is the runner's last pre-off print: settle at win_lose from racing_bsp, else charge -£10. Bands on the entry print: <3, 3-6, 6-12, 12-20, ALL<=20. One entry per race per class per band: largest move, ties by name. FOLLOW = back SHORT, lay DRIFT; FADE = lay SHORT, back DRIFT. 40 gated lines, halves by date median, house bar.

Rule fixed before prices: MATCH_ODDS and OVER_UNDER_25, every runner, every month with official results, one market per event, last non-in-play print T-20 to T-2 min, never earlier. State on that raw print, for B in 2, 3, 4, 6, 10: AT = B exactly; BELOW = one or two ticks under; ABOVE = one or two ticks over. Back and lay, worse of the print and the next within 120 s, one tick adverse, 2%, £10 risk, to the official result. 60 lines. Standard bar, t on per-game sums shown, halves by date median. Shown beside each AT line: AT less BELOW and ABOVE pooled, Welch t. Prices are prints, so a pass is an upper bound.

DONE 2026-11-06 — nothing passes, 0 of 24; best LONE DOG 2.5-4 back n 2,113 +0.346 t 1.06; reports/finder_2026-11-06_items257-265.md (jobs/499_ladder_slot.sh; Finder's own, source c; same tables; item 208 read Saturday 3pm on English and Scottish short favourites only) **Crowded kick-off slot v a lone game.** Rationale: with many games starting together, attention and matching are spread thin, so a price may be staler than in a game kicking off alone; item 218 read the same idea in racing. Rule fixed before prices: slot size = distinct event name at the exact market time, counted across all soccer MO markets on the tape; CROWD = top fifth of games by slot size, LONE = bottom fifth, cuts printed, MID the control, which cannot pass. Entry as item 260. Legs: FAV = shorter non-draw MO runner, bands <=1.5, 1.5-2.5; DOG = longer non-draw runner, bands 2.5-4, 4-10; OVER and UNDER on OU2.5. Both non-draw runners need a print, ties no bet. Back and lay, 24 scored lines. Same costs, bar and halves.

DONE 2026-11-06 — nothing passes, 0 of 8; best DNB G2 CHEAP back n 179 +2.078 t 2.66 h1 +1.816 h2 +2.416; DNB G2 DEAR lay n 279 +1.229 t 1.70; follow-ups items 264, 265; reports/finder_2026-11-06_items257-265.md (jobs/499_derivative_gap.sh; Finder's own, source c; tables bf_hist_other, bf_hist_result; items 111, 113, 157, 164 read these markets alone, never against Match Odds on official results) **Draw No Bet and Double Chance against the price Match Odds implies.** Rationale: both are sums of Match Odds outcomes and thinner, and the 3 Nov lag read says DNB closes only about 11% of its gap a minute. Rule fixed before prices: every month with official results, one market per event per type, last non-in-play print T-20 to T-2 on all three MO runners and the derivative runner, each MO print within 600 s of the derivative print, MO book 0.98 to 1.08. Fair from MO normalised: DNB home = H/(H+A); Home or Draw = H+D; Draw or Away = D+A; Home or Away = H+A. Gap g = fair less 1/print. Per game per line, the runner with the largest gap. G2 = 0.02 to 0.04, G4 = 0.04 to 0.10. CHEAP (g > 0) back, DEAR (g < 0) lay: 2 markets x 2 bands x 2 = 8 scored lines. Worse of the print and the next within 120 s, one tick adverse, 2%, £10 risk, to the official result. A drawn game voids DNB: no result row there and MO's winner the Draw counts as a trade at £0, never dropped; other missing rows dropped and counted. Standard bar, halves by date median. Cannot pass, shown: G10 (g 0.10 or more, stale), the same bands with the sides swapped, NEAR (gap under 0.01), and the taker's fills only (back on a down-print, lay on an up-print).
264. DONE 2026-11-06 — pilot, a taker cannot have it: 0 of 130 games show the 2 points at the touch, median DNB spread 4.51 points, 29 of 260 runners show it at mid; only 130 of 1,215 games had a usable row; reports/finder_2026-11-06_items257-265.md (jobs/499_dnb_touch.sh; Finder's own, source a; table liq_sample, local copy, about 6 days) **Item 263's DNB gap at the real touch: is the 2 points there to take?** Rationale: 263 DNB G2 CHEAP back n 179 +2.078 t 2.66 h1 +1.816 h2 +2.416, DEAR lay n 279 +1.229 t 1.70, both on prints; the 3 Nov read said the half-spread is wider than the gap. A pilot that cannot pass on 6 days. Rule fixed before these prices are read: MO and DNB, last row at minute 0 at least 120 s before the game's first row at minute 1 or later, all three MO runners and both DNB runners two-sided within 120 s of each other. Fair from MO mids normalised, book 0.98 to 1.08. CHEAP = fair less 1/back_px >= 0.02, back at back_px; DEAR = 1/lay_px less fair >= 0.02, lay at lay_px; each no more than 0.10; largest gap per game per side. One tick adverse, 2%, £10 risk. Result from MO's last row: one runner's ltp <= 1.1 wins; the Draw voids DNB at £0, counted as a trade; no clear winner is unresolved, counted, never scored. Shown: how many games show the gap at mid against at the touch, and the median DNB spread in points. Halves by date.

284. RUNNING 2026-11-06 (Finder's own, source c; tables bf_hist_other, bf_hist_result, af_fixtures, af_price_map; job 499_cup_ties) **Cup-style ties between divisions, and summer friendlies, before the off.** Rationale: 300 ideas on the list and none tests a game outside the league tables; a cup tie brings a name and a story, a friendly brings rotation the price may not hold. Rule fixed before prices: a Betfair team name is tied to an af team through mapped games, its league is that of its nearest af_fixtures game within 60 days of the event, UEFA ignored; youth and women out. Event with both sides known, and no af_fixtures game for that pair within 2 days. Classes: DIVS, same country, different tier (ENG 39 40 41 42, GER 78 79, FRA 61 62, ITA 135 136, ESP 140 141), Sep to May; SAME, same league id, Sep to May; SUMMER, any two known sides, 15 Jun to 20 Aug; OTHERS, different country, Sep to May. Entry and fills as item 274: last print T-20 to T-2, worse of it and the next within 120 s, one tick, 2%, £10, over 50 not traded, official result, none dropped and counted, second market id skipped. Lines: DIVS, DIVS gap 2+, DIVS lower side at home, each LOWER, HIGHER, DRAW, back and lay; SAME, SUMMER, OTHERS HOME, DRAW, AWAY back and lay; DIVS, SAME, SUMMER, OTHERS Over 2.5 and Under 2.5 back and lay. League games in the same months, a sample, are the control, not scored. Standard bar, halves by date. Limit: the league cups and Conference League read as one, and a summer tie may be a qualifier.
285. RUNNING 2026-11-06 (Finder's own, source d; tables af_fixtures, af_price_map, bf_hist_price; job 499_table_v_price) **League table against the price.** Rationale: form, Elo, xG luck and dead rubbers are tested, a plain place in the table is not, and it is what a casual punter reads. Rule fixed before prices: tables from finished games before kick-off, 3 points a win, then goal difference, ties at the same time not history for each other; leagues 39 40 41 42 61 62 78 79 88 94 119 135 136 140 141 144 179 197 203 207 218 only, as the rest split seasons or conferences; both sides 10+ games and a league season of 10+ teams; a game past the mode of games played is dropped. Entry as jobs/499_referee_home.sh: earliest real MO print in tape minutes 0-2, its guards, one tick, 2%, £10, to the score. HIGHER is the side higher in the table. DIS means HIGHER has the longer price. Lines: DIS_H (HIGHER at home) and DIS_A (HIGHER away), place gap 4+ and 8+, HIGHER and LOWER, back and lay; TOP3 v BOTTOM3 HIGHER and LOWER, back and lay. Control, all games, home and away back and lay, not scored. Standard bar, halves by date.
286. RUNNING 2026-11-06 (Finder's own, source a; tables bf_hist_other, cricket_matches on Railway, that table only; job 499_cricket_toss) **The T20 toss at the price just before the off.** Rationale: the toss is the one large public fact right before a T20; item 126 read shocks without knowing who won it, cricket_matches names the winner and the choice for 1,750 games. Rule fixed before prices: T20 games matched to a two-runner MATCH_ODDS market by normalised names within a day; last non-in-play print T-20 to T-2, worse of it and the next within 120 s, one tick, 2%, £10, over 50 not traded, one bet per game, official winner; no winner dropped and counted, also read at zero P&L. Lines back and lay: toss winner; winner who fielded; winner who batted; side batting second; side batting first. Context only, not tradable: the same side's last print T-120 to T-45, to size how far the toss moved it. Standard bar, halves by date. Limit: if a market's time is not the real start, an entry may fall before the toss. These games helped read item 271's months, not this rule.
